    { name: 'location', label: 'Location or meeting link' },
    { name: 'meetingUrl', label: 'Meeting URL', type: 'url' },
    { name: 'contactId', label: 'Contact', relation: 'contact' },
    { name: 'applicationId', label: 'Application', relation: 'application' },
    { name: 'notes', label: 'Notes', type: 'textarea' },
  ],
}

const labels: Record<RecordType, string> = {
  company: 'company',
  application: 'application',
  contact: 'contact',
  task: 'task',
  event: 'event',
}

function getCurrentRecord(type: RecordType, id: string | undefined, data: ReturnType<typeof useCareerData>['data']): EditableRecord | undefined {
  if (!id) return undefined
  if (type === 'company') return data.companies.find((item) => item.id === id)
  if (type === 'application') return data.applications.find((item) => item.id === id)
  if (type === 'contact') return data.contacts.find((item) => item.id === id)
  if (type === 'task') return data.tasks.find((item) => item.id === id)
  return data.events.find((item) => item.id === id)
}

function getRecordField(record: EditableRecord | undefined, field: string): unknown {
  if (!record || !(field in record)) return undefined
  return record[field as keyof EditableRecord]
}

function inputValue(value: unknown, type: Field['type']) {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') return ''
  if (type === 'date' || type === 'datetime-local') {
    const date = new Date(value)
    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return ''
    const localDate = new Date(date.getTime() - date.getTimezoneOffset() * 60_000).toISOString()
    return type === 'date' ? localDate.slice(0, 10) : localDate.slice(0, 16)
  }
  return value
}

export function RecordDialog({ type, recordId, initialDate, onClose }: RecordDialogProps) {
  const { data, addRecord, updateRecord } = useCareerData()
  const record = getCurrentRecord(type, recordId, data)
  const fields = fieldsByType[type]
  const save = (formData: FormData) => {
    const values = Object.fromEntries(fields.map((field) => [field.name, String(formData.get(field.name) ?? '')]))
    const required = fields.find((field) => field.required)
    const validator = z.object({ [required?.name ?? '']: z.string().trim().min(1, `Enter a ${labels[type]} ${required?.name === 'name' ? 'name' : required?.name === 'title' ? 'title' : 'job title'}`) })
    const result = validator.safeParse(values)
    if (!result.success) {
      document.getElementById(`error-${type}`)?.replaceChildren(result.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'Check the required fields.')
      return
    }
    if (recordId) updateRecord(type, recordId, values)
    else addRecord(type, values)
    onClose()
  }

  return (
    <div className="dialog-backdrop" role="presentation" onMouseDown={(event) => { if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose() }}>
      <section className="dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="dialog-title">
        <div className="dialog-heading">
          <div>
            <p className="eyebrow">{recordId ? 'Edit record' : 'New record'}</p>
            <h2 id="dialog-title">{recordId ? 'Update' : 'Add'} {labels[type]}</h2>
          </div>
          <button className="icon-button" type="button" aria-label="Close dialog" onClick={onClose}>×</button>
        </div>
        <form action={(formData) => save(formData)}>
          <div className="form-grid">
            {fields.map((field) => (
              <label className={field.type === 'textarea' ? 'form-field full-width' : 'form-field'} key={field.name}>
                <span>{field.label}{field.required ? ' *' : ''}</span>
                {field.company ? (
                  <select name={field.name} required={field.required} defaultValue={String(getRecordField(record, field.name) ?? '')}>
                    <option value="">No company</option>
                    {data.companies.map((company) => <option key={company.id} value={company.id}>{company.name}</option>)}
                  </select>
                ) : field.relation === 'contact' ? (
                  <select name={field.name} defaultValue={String(getRecordField(record, field.name) ?? '')}>
                    <option value="">No contact</option>
                    {data.contacts.map((contact) => <option key={contact.id} value={contact.id}>{contact.name}</option>)}
                  </select>
                ) : field.relation === 'application' ? (
                  <select name={field.name} defaultValue={String(getRecordField(record, field.name) ?? '')}>
                    <option value="">No application</option>
                    {data.applications.map((application) => <option key={application.id} value={application.id}>{application.jobTitle}</option>)}
                  </select>
                ) : field.type === 'select' ? (
                  <select name={field.name} defaultValue={String(getRecordField(record, field.name) ?? field.options?.[0] ?? '')}>
                    {field.options?.map((option) => <option key={option}>{option}</option>)}
                  </select>
                ) : field.type === 'textarea' ? (
                  <textarea name={field.name} defaultValue={inputValue(getRecordField(record, field.name), field.type)} />
                ) : (
                  <input
                    name={field.name}
                    type={field.type ?? 'text'}
                    required={field.required}
                    defaultValue={inputValue(getRecordField(record, field.name), field.type) || (!record && initialDate && field.name === 'dueAt' ? format(initialDate, 'yyyy-MM-dd') : !record && initialDate && field.name === 'startsAt' ? format(initialDate, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm") : '')}
                    placeholder={field.placeholder}
                  />
                )}
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>
          <p id={`error-${type}`} className="form-error" role="alert" />
          <div className="dialog-actions">
            <button className="button button-secondary" type="button" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
            <button className="button button-primary" type="submit">{recordId ? 'Save changes' : `Add ${labels[type]}`}</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/context/CareerData.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { z } from 'zod'
import { demoData } from '../data/demo'
import { CareerDataContext } from './careerDataContext'
import type { CareerDataContextValue } from './careerDataContext'
import type { CareerData } from '../types'

const storageKey = 'careeros-demo-v1'
const storedDateSchema = z.string().refine((value) => value === '' || !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value)))
const companySchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(), name: z.string(), industry: z.string(), location: z.string(), website: z.string(),
  size: z.string(), tags: z.array(z.string()), careersUrl: z.string(), linkedinUrl: z.string(),
  notes: z.string(), createdAt: storedDateSchema,
})
const applicationSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(), companyId: z.string(), jobTitle: z.string(),
  status: z.enum(['Saved', 'Applied', 'Assessment', 'Interview', 'Offer', 'Rejected']),
  appliedAt: storedDateSchema, nextAction: z.string(), nextFollowUpAt: storedDateSchema, location: z.string(),
  jobDescription: z.string(), jobUrl: z.string(), applicationUrl: z.string(), jobType: z.string(),
  workArrangement: z.string(), resumeReference: z.string(), notes: z.string(),
})
const contactSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(), companyId: z.string(), name: z.string(), jobTitle: z.string(),
  status: z.string(), lastContactedAt: storedDateSchema, tags: z.array(z.string()),
  linkedinUrl: z.string(), email: z.string(), phone: z.string(),
  connectionRequestedAt: storedDateSchema, connectionAcceptedAt: storedDateSchema,
  nextFollowUpAt: storedDateSchema, notes: z.string(),
})
const taskSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(), title: z.string(), companyId: z.string(), dueAt: storedDateSchema,
  priority: z.enum(['Low', 'Medium', 'High']), done: z.boolean(),
  description: z.string(), contactId: z.string(), applicationId: z.string(), category: z.string(),
})
const eventSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(), title: z.string(), eventType: z.string(), companyId: z.string(),
  startsAt: storedDateSchema, location: z.string(), endsAt: storedDateSchema, meetingUrl: z.string(),
  notes: z.string(), contactId: z.string(), applicationId: z.string(),
})
const activitySchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(), description: z.string(), occurredAt: storedDateSchema, companyId: z.string(),
})
const careerDataSchema = z.object({
  companies: z.array(companySchema),
  applications: z.array(applicationSchema),
  contacts: z.array(contactSchema),
  tasks: z.array(taskSchema),
  events: z.array(eventSchema),
  activities: z.array(activitySchema),
})

function readStoredData(): CareerData {
  try {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem(storageKey)
    if (!stored) return demoData
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(stored)
    const result = careerDataSchema.safeParse(parsed)
    if (result.success) return result.data
    console.warn('CareerOS demo data did not match the current format. Starting with fresh sample data.')
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('CareerOS could not read saved demo data. Starting with fresh sample data.', error)
  }
  return demoData
}

function dateValue(value: string | undefined, fallback: string) {
  if (!value) return fallback
  const parsed = new Date(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value) ? `${value}T00:00:00` : value)
  return Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? fallback : parsed.toISOString()
}

export function DemoCareerDataProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<CareerData>(readStoredData)

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(data))
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('CareerOS could not persist demo data in this browser.', error)
    }
  }, [data])

  const value = useMemo<CareerDataContextValue>(() => {
    const addRecord: CareerDataContextValue['addRecord'] = (type, fields) => {
      const id = `${type}-${crypto.randomUUID()}`
      const now = new Date().toISOString()
      const companyId = fields.companyId ?? ''
      setData((current) => {
        switch (type) {
          case 'company':
            return { ...current, companies: [{ id, name: fields.name.trim(), industry: fields.industry, location: fields.location, website: fields.website, size: fields.size, tags: fields.tags.split(',').map((tag) => tag.trim()).filter(Boolean), careersUrl: fields.careersUrl, linkedinUrl: fields.linkedinUrl, notes: fields.notes, createdAt: now }, ...current.companies] }
          case 'application':
            return { ...current, applications: [{ id, companyId, jobTitle: fields.jobTitle.trim(), status: fields.status as CareerData['applications'][number]['status'], appliedAt: dateValue(fields.appliedAt, now), nextAction: fields.nextAction, nextFollowUpAt: dateValue(fields.nextFollowUpAt, ''), location: fields.location, jobDescription: fields.jobDescription, jobUrl: fields.jobUrl, applicationUrl: fields.applicationUrl, jobType: fields.jobType, workArrangement: fields.workArrangement, resumeReference: fields.resumeReference, notes: fields.notes }, ...current.applications] }
          case 'contact':
            return { ...current, contacts: [{ id, companyId, name: fields.name.trim(), jobTitle: fields.jobTitle, status: fields.status, lastContactedAt: dateValue(fields.lastContactedAt, now), tags: fields.tags.split(',').map((tag) => tag.trim()).filter(Boolean), linkedinUrl: fields.linkedinUrl, email: fields.email, phone: fields.phone, connectionRequestedAt: dateValue(fields.connectionRequestedAt, ''), connectionAcceptedAt: dateValue(fields.connectionAcceptedAt, ''), nextFollowUpAt: dateValue(fields.nextFollowUpAt, ''), notes: fields.notes }, ...current.contacts] }
          case 'task':
            return { ...current, tasks: [{ id, title: fields.title.trim(), companyId, dueAt: dateValue(fields.dueAt, now), priority: fields.priority as CareerData['tasks'][number]['priority'], done: false, description: fields.description, contactId: fields.contactId, applicationId: fields.applicationId, category: fields.category }, ...current.tasks] }
          case 'event':
            return { ...current, events: [{ id, title: fields.title.trim(), eventType: fields.eventType, companyId, startsAt: dateValue(fields.startsAt, now), location: fields.location, endsAt: dateValue(fields.endsAt, ''), meetingUrl: fields.meetingUrl, notes: fields.notes, contactId: fields.contactId, applicationId: fields.applicationId }, ...current.events] }
        }
      })
    }

    const updateRecord: CareerDataContextValue['updateRecord'] = (type, id, fields) => {
      setData((current) => {
        switch (type) {
          case 'company':
            return { ...current, companies: current.companies.map((item) => item.id === id ? { ...item, name: fields.name.trim(), industry: fields.industry, location: fields.location, website: fields.website, size: fields.size, tags: fields.tags.split(',').map((tag) => tag.trim()).filter(Boolean), careersUrl: fields.careersUrl, linkedinUrl: fields.linkedinUrl, notes: fields.notes } : item) }
          case 'application':
            return { ...current, applications: current.applications.map((item) => item.id === id ? { ...item, companyId: fields.companyId, jobTitle: fields.jobTitle.trim(), status: fields.status as CareerData['applications'][number]['status'], appliedAt: dateValue(fields.appliedAt, item.appliedAt), nextAction: fields.nextAction, nextFollowUpAt: dateValue(fields.nextFollowUpAt, ''), location: fields.location, jobDescription: fields.jobDescription, jobUrl: fields.jobUrl, applicationUrl: fields.applicationUrl, jobType: fields.jobType, workArrangement: fields.workArrangement, resumeReference: fields.resumeReference, notes: fields.notes } : item) }
          case 'contact':
            return { ...current, contacts: current.contacts.map((item) => item.id === id ? { ...item, companyId: fields.companyId, name: fields.name.trim(), jobTitle: fields.jobTitle, status: fields.status, lastContactedAt: dateValue(fields.lastContactedAt, item.lastContactedAt), tags: fields.tags.split(',').map((tag) => tag.trim()).filter(Boolean), linkedinUrl: fields.linkedinUrl, email: fields.email, phone: fields.phone, connectionRequestedAt: dateValue(fields.connectionRequestedAt, ''), connectionAcceptedAt: dateValue(fields.connectionAcceptedAt, ''), nextFollowUpAt: dateValue(fields.nextFollowUpAt, ''), notes: fields.notes } : item) }
          case 'task':
            return { ...current, tasks: current.tasks.map((item) => item.id === id ? { ...item, title: fields.title.trim(), companyId: fields.companyId, dueAt: dateValue(fields.dueAt, item.dueAt), priority: fields.priority as CareerData['tasks'][number]['priority'], description: fields.description, contactId: fields.contactId, applicationId: fields.applicationId, category: fields.category } : item) }
          case 'event':
            return { ...current, events: current.events.map((item) => item.id === id ? { ...item, title: fields.title.trim(), eventType: fields.eventType, companyId: fields.companyId, startsAt: dateValue(fields.startsAt, item.startsAt), location: fields.location, endsAt: dateValue(fields.endsAt, ''), meetingUrl: fields.meetingUrl, notes: fields.notes, contactId: fields.contactId, applicationId: fields.applicationId } : item) }
        }
      })
    }

    const deleteRecord: CareerDataContextValue['deleteRecord'] = (type, id) => {
      setData((current) => {
        if (type === 'company') return {
          ...current,
          companies: current.companies.filter((item) => item.id !== id),
          applications: current.applications.map((item) => item.companyId === id ? { ...item, companyId: '' } : item),
          contacts: current.contacts.map((item) => item.companyId === id ? { ...item, companyId: '' } : item),
          tasks: current.tasks.map((item) => item.companyId === id ? { ...item, companyId: '' } : item),
          events: current.events.map((item) => item.companyId === id ? { ...item, companyId: '' } : item),
        }
        if (type === 'application') return {
          ...current,
          applications: current.applications.filter((item) => item.id !== id),
          tasks: current.tasks.map((item) => item.applicationId === id ? { ...item, applicationId: '' } : item),
          events: current.events.map((item) => item.applicationId === id ? { ...item, applicationId: '' } : item),
        }
        if (type === 'contact') return {
          ...current,
          contacts: current.contacts.filter((item) => item.id !== id),
          tasks: current.tasks.map((item) => item.contactId === id ? { ...item, contactId: '' } : item),
          events: current.events.map((item) => item.contactId === id ? { ...item, contactId: '' } : item),
        }
        if (type === 'task') return { ...current, tasks: current.tasks.filter((item) => item.id !== id) }
        return { ...current, events: current.events.filter((item) => item.id !== id) }
      })
    }

    const toggleTask = (id: string) => setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      tasks: current.tasks.map((task) => task.id === id ? { ...task, done: !task.done } : task),
    }))
    return { data, addRecord, updateRecord, deleteRecord, toggleTask }
  }, [data])

  return <CareerDataContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</CareerDataContext.Provider>
}

import { createContext } from 'react'
import type { CareerData, RecordType } from '../types'

export interface CareerDataContextValue {
  data: CareerData
  addRecord: (type: RecordType, values: Record<string, string>) => void
  updateRecord: (type: RecordType, id: string, values: Record<string, string>) => void
  deleteRecord: (type: RecordType, id: string) => void
  toggleTask: (id: string) => void
}

export const CareerDataContext = createContext<CareerDataContextValue | null>(null)

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { ThemeContext } from './themeContext'

export type Theme = 'light' | 'dark'

const themeStorageKey = 'careeros-theme-v1'

function readTheme(): Theme {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(themeStorageKey) === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('CareerOS could not read the saved theme preference.', error)
    return 'light'
  }
}

export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>(readTheme)

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(themeStorageKey, theme)
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('CareerOS could not persist the theme preference.', error)
    }
  }, [theme])

  const value = useMemo(() => ({
    theme,
    toggleTheme: () => setTheme((current) => current === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light'),
  }), [theme])

  return <ThemeContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ThemeContext.Provider>
}

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { demoData, getCompanyName } from './demo'

describe('demo data', () => {
  it('starts with a useful sample workspace and all application statuses', () => {
    expect(demoData.companies).toHaveLength(7)
    expect(demoData.applications.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(10)
    expect(new Set(demoData.applications.map((application) => application.status)).size).toBe(6)
    expect(demoData.contacts.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(8)
    expect(demoData.tasks.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(6)
    expect(demoData.events.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3)
  })

  it('uses valid company references and a clear fallback for removed companies', () => {
    const companyIds = new Set(demoData.companies.map((company) => company.id))
    for (const record of [...demoData.applications, ...demoData.contacts, ...demoData.tasks, ...demoData.events]) {
      expect(companyIds.has(record.companyId)).toBe(true)
    }
    const contactIds = new Set(demoData.contacts.map((contact) => contact.id))
    const applicationIds = new Set(demoData.applications.map((application) => application.id))
    expect(demoData.tasks.every((task) => !task.contactId || contactIds.has(task.contactId))).toBe(true)
    expect(demoData.tasks.every((task) => !task.applicationId || applicationIds.has(task.applicationId))).toBe(true)
    expect(demoData.events.every((event) => !event.contactId || contactIds.has(event.contactId))).toBe(true)
    expect(demoData.events.every((event) => !event.applicationId || applicationIds.has(event.applicationId))).toBe(true)
    expect(getCompanyName(demoData, 'missing-company')).toBe('Independent')
  })
})

export const demoData: CareerData = { companies, applications, contacts, tasks, events, activities }

export const getCompanyName = (data: CareerData, companyId: string) =>
  data.companies.find((company) => company.id === companyId)?.name ?? 'Independent'

export const recordCollections = {
  companies: (data: CareerData) => data.companies,
  applications: (data: CareerData) => data.applications,
  contacts: (data: CareerData) => data.contacts,
  tasks: (data: CareerData) => data.tasks,
  events: (data: CareerData) => data.events,
} satisfies Record<string, (data: CareerData) => Array<Company | Application | Contact | FollowUpTask | CareerEvent>>

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { isSameDay } from 'date-fns'
import { demoData } from '../data/demo'
import { getCalendarDays, getItemsForDate, getScheduledItems } from './calendar'

describe('monthly calendar helpers', () => {
  it('creates a complete Monday-first grid for leap February', () => {
    const days = getCalendarDays(new Date(2024, 1, 1))
    expect(days.length % 7).toBe(0)
    expect(days.some((day) => isSameDay(day, new Date(2024, 1, 29)))).toBe(true)
    expect(days[0]?.getDay()).toBe(1)
  })

  it('groups local events and unfinished tasks by their scheduled date', () => {
    const date = '2024-02-29T12:30:00.000Z'
    const data = {
      ...demoData,
      companies: [{ ...demoData.companies[0]!, id: 'company-1', name: 'Example' }],
      events: [{ ...demoData.events[0]!, id: 'event-1', companyId: 'company-1', startsAt: date }],
      tasks: [
        { ...demoData.tasks[0]!, id: 'task-1', companyId: 'company-1', dueAt: date, done: false },
        { ...demoData.tasks[0]!, id: 'task-2', companyId: 'company-1', dueAt: date, done: true },
      ],
    }
    const items = getScheduledItems(data)
    const selected = getItemsForDate(items, new Date(2024, 1, 29))

    expect(items).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(selected.map((item) => item.type).sort()).toEqual(['event', 'task'])
    expect(selected.every((item) => item.company === 'Example')).toBe(true)
  })
})



----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/lib/calendar.ts
SIZE: 1896 bytes
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { addDays, endOfMonth, endOfWeek, isSameDay, isValid, parseISO, startOfMonth, startOfWeek } from 'date-fns'
import type { CareerData } from '../types'

export interface CalendarItem {
  id: string
  type: 'event' | 'task'
  title: string
  date: Date
  time: string
  category: string
  company: string
  location: string
}

export function getCalendarDays(month: Date) {
  const firstDay = startOfWeek(startOfMonth(month), { weekStartsOn: 1 })
  const lastDay = endOfWeek(endOfMonth(month), { weekStartsOn: 1 })
  const days: Date[] = []

  for (let day = firstDay; day <= lastDay; day = addDays(day, 1)) days.push(day)
  return days
}

export function getScheduledItems(data: CareerData): CalendarItem[] {
  const companyNames = new Map(data.companies.map((company) => [company.id, company.name]))
  const events: CalendarItem[] = data.events.flatMap((event) => {
    const date = parseISO(event.startsAt)
    if (!isValid(date)) return []
    return [{
      id: event.id,
      type: 'event',
      title: event.title,
      date,
      time: event.startsAt,
      category: event.eventType,
      company: companyNames.get(event.companyId) ?? '',
      location: event.location,
    }]
  })
  const tasks: CalendarItem[] = data.tasks.flatMap((task) => {
    if (task.done || !task.dueAt) return []
    const date = parseISO(task.dueAt)
    if (!isValid(date)) return []
    return [{
      id: task.id,
      type: 'task',
      title: task.title,
      date,
      time: '',
      category: 'Task',
      company: companyNames.get(task.companyId) ?? '',
      location: '',
    }]
  })

  return [...events, ...tasks].sort((first, second) => first.date.getTime() - second.date.getTime())
}

export function getItemsForDate(items: CalendarItem[], date: Date) {
  return items.filter((item) => isSameDay(item.date, date))
}

FILE: src/lib/followUps.test.ts
SIZE: 1588 bytes

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { addDays, startOfDay } from 'date-fns'
import { demoData } from '../data/demo'
import { getUpcomingFollowUps } from './followUps'

describe('upcoming follow-ups', () => {
  it('combines task, application, and contact follow-ups without same-date duplicates', () => {
    const today = startOfDay(new Date())
    const dueDate = addDays(today, 2).toISOString()
    const data = {
      ...demoData,
      tasks: [{ ...demoData.tasks[0]!, id: 'task-one', applicationId: 'app-1', contactId: 'ct-1', dueAt: dueDate, done: false }],
      applications: [
        { ...demoData.applications[0]!, id: 'app-1', nextFollowUpAt: dueDate },
        { ...demoData.applications[1]!, id: 'app-2', nextFollowUpAt: dueDate },
      ],
      contacts: [
        { ...demoData.contacts[0]!, id: 'ct-1', nextFollowUpAt: dueDate },
        { ...demoData.contacts[1]!, id: 'ct-2', nextFollowUpAt: dueDate },
      ],
    }

    const actions = getUpcomingFollowUps(data)

    expect(actions.map(({ recordType, id }) => `${recordType}:${id}`)).toEqual([
      'task:task-one',
      'application:app-2',
      'contact:ct-2',
    ])
  })

  it('does not count completed tasks or rejected applications', () => {
    const data = {
      ...demoData,
      tasks: [{ ...demoData.tasks[0]!, done: true }],
      applications: [{ ...demoData.applications[0]!, status: 'Rejected' as const, nextFollowUpAt: new Date().toISOString() }],
      contacts: [],
    }
    expect(getUpcomingFollowUps(data)).toEqual([])
  })
})



----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src/lib/followUps.ts
SIZE: 2531 bytes
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { isSameDay, isValid, parseISO } from 'date-fns'
import type { CareerData, RecordType, TaskPriority } from '../types'

export interface FollowUpAction {
  id: string
  recordType: Extract<RecordType, 'task' | 'application' | 'contact'>
  title: string
  companyId: string
  dueAt: string
  priority: TaskPriority | null
}

function validDate(value: string) {
  if (!value) return undefined
  const date = parseISO(value)
  return isValid(date) ? date : undefined
}

export function getUpcomingFollowUps(data: CareerData): FollowUpAction[] {
  const tasks = data.tasks.filter((task) => !task.done && validDate(task.dueAt))
  const taskForApplicationOnDate = (applicationId: string, date: Date) => data.tasks.some((task) => {
    if (task.applicationId !== applicationId) return false
    const taskDate = validDate(task.dueAt)
    return Boolean(taskDate && isSameDay(taskDate, date))
  })
  const taskForContactOnDate = (contactId: string, date: Date) => data.tasks.some((task) => {
    if (task.contactId !== contactId) return false
    const taskDate = validDate(task.dueAt)
    return Boolean(taskDate && isSameDay(taskDate, date))
  })

  const taskActions: FollowUpAction[] = tasks.map((task) => ({
    id: task.id,
    recordType: 'task',
    title: task.title,
    companyId: task.companyId,
    dueAt: task.dueAt,
    priority: task.priority,
  }))
  const applicationActions: FollowUpAction[] = data.applications.flatMap((application) => {
    const date = validDate(application.nextFollowUpAt)
    if (!date || application.status === 'Rejected' || taskForApplicationOnDate(application.id, date)) return []
    return [{
      id: application.id,
      recordType: 'application' as const,
      title: application.nextAction || `Follow up: ${application.jobTitle}`,
      companyId: application.companyId,
      dueAt: application.nextFollowUpAt,
      priority: null,
    }]
  })
  const contactActions: FollowUpAction[] = data.contacts.flatMap((contact) => {
    const date = validDate(contact.nextFollowUpAt)
    if (!date || taskForContactOnDate(contact.id, date)) return []
    return [{
      id: contact.id,
      recordType: 'contact' as const,
      title: `Follow up with ${contact.name}`,
      companyId: contact.companyId,
      dueAt: contact.nextFollowUpAt,
      priority: null,
    }]
  })

  return [...taskActions, ...applicationActions, ...contactActions]
    .sort((first, second) => first.dueAt.localeCompare(second.dueAt))
}

import { useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { addDays, format, isBefore, parseISO, startOfDay } from 'date-fns'
import { ArrowRight, BriefcaseBusiness, CalendarClock, Check, Clock3, Plus } from 'lucide-react'
import { useNavigate, useOutletContext } from 'react-router-dom'
import { getCompanyName } from '../data/demo'
import { useCareerData } from '../context/useCareerData'
import { MonthlyCalendar } from '../components/MonthlyCalendar'
import { RecordDialog } from '../components/RecordDialog'
import { getUpcomingFollowUps } from '../lib/followUps'
import type { AppShellContext, RecordType } from '../types'

const dateLabel = (value: string) => value ? format(parseISO(value), 'MMM d') : '—'
const activeStatuses = ['Saved', 'Applied', 'Assessment', 'Interview']

export function Dashboard() {
  const { data, toggleTask, deleteRecord } = useCareerData()
  const { openAdd } = useOutletContext<AppShellContext>()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [now] = useState(() => new Date())
  const [editRecord, setEditRecord] = useState<{ type: RecordType; id: string } | null>(null)
  const today = startOfDay(now)
  const active = data.applications.filter((application) => activeStatuses.includes(application.status)).length
  const upcomingInterviews = data.events.filter((event) => event.eventType === 'Interview' && parseISO(event.startsAt) >= now).length
  const followUps = useMemo(() => getUpcomingFollowUps(data), [data])
  const upcomingActions = followUps.slice(0, 5)
  const followUpsDue = followUps.filter((action) => isBefore(parseISO(action.dueAt), addDays(today, 1))).length
  const recentApplications = useMemo(() => data.applications
    .slice()
    .sort((first, second) => second.appliedAt.localeCompare(first.appliedAt))
    .slice(0, 5), [data.applications])
  const greeting = now.getHours() < 12 ? 'Good morning' : now.getHours() < 18 ? 'Good afternoon' : 'Good evening'

  const openCalendarItem = (type: RecordType, id: string) => setEditRecord({ type, id })
  const deleteEvent = (id: string) => {
    if (window.confirm('Delete this event? This action cannot be undone.')) deleteRecord('event', id)
  }

  return <div className="dashboard-page">
    <section className="welcome-row dashboard-welcome">
      <div><p className="eyebrow">{format(now, 'EEEE, MMMM d')}</p><h1>{greeting}, Alex <span className="greeting-mark">✳</span></h1><p className="welcome-subtitle">Your next steps and schedule, in one place.</p></div>
      <button className="button button-primary" onClick={() => openAdd('application')}><Plus size={16} /> Add application</button>
    </section>

    <section className="metrics-grid" aria-label="Career overview">
      <MetricCard label="Total applications" value={data.applications.length} icon={<BriefcaseBusiness size={16} />} tone="violet" />
      <MetricCard label="Active applications" value={active} icon={<ArrowRight size={16} />} tone="blue" />
      <MetricCard label="Upcoming interviews" value={upcomingInterviews} icon={<CalendarClock size={16} />} tone="green" />
      <MetricCard label="Follow-ups due" value={followUpsDue} icon={<Clock3 size={16} />} tone="amber" />
    </section>

    <section className="dashboard-focus-grid">
      <article className="panel dashboard-calendar-panel">
        <MonthlyCalendar
          data={data}
          compact
          onAdd={openAdd}
          onOpen={openCalendarItem}
          onDeleteEvent={deleteEvent}
        />
      </article>
      <article className="panel dashboard-actions-panel">
        <PanelHeading title="Your next steps" subtitle="Open tasks and follow-ups" trailing={<button className="text-action" onClick={() => openAdd('task')}>Add task <Plus size={13} /></button>} />
        {upcomingActions.length ? <div className="task-list">
          {upcomingActions.map((action) => {
            const overdue = isBefore(parseISO(action.dueAt), today)
            return <div className={`task-row ${overdue ? 'task-overdue' : ''}`} key={`${action.recordType}-${action.id}`}>
              {action.recordType === 'task' ? <button className="task-check" aria-label="Mark task complete" onClick={() => toggleTask(action.id)}><Check size={12} /></button> : <span className="followup-action-symbol" aria-hidden="true"><Clock3 size={13} /></span>}
              <button className="task-copy task-open-button" onClick={() => openCalendarItem(action.recordType, action.id)}><strong>{action.title}</strong><span>{getCompanyName(data, action.companyId)}</span></button>
              <div className="task-meta"><span className={action.priority ? `priority priority-${action.priority.toLowerCase()}` : 'followup-label'}>{action.priority ?? 'Follow-up'}</span><time>{overdue ? 'Overdue' : dateLabel(action.dueAt)}</time></div>
            </div>
          })}
        </div> : <EmptyState text="No open follow-ups. Add a task to plan your next move." />}
        <button className="panel-footer-action" onClick={() => navigate('/tasks')}>View all tasks <ArrowRight size={14} /></button>
      </article>
    </section>

    <article className="panel applications-panel dashboard-recent-panel">
      <PanelHeading title="Recent applications" subtitle="Your latest opportunities" trailing={<button className="text-action" onClick={() => navigate('/applications')}>View all <ArrowRight size={14} /></button>} />
      {recentApplications.length ? <div className="application-table-wrap"><table className="application-table">
        <thead><tr><th>Company / role</th><th>Date applied</th><th>Status</th><th>Next step</th><th /></tr></thead>
        <tbody>{recentApplications.map((application) => (
          <tr key={application.id} onClick={() => setEditRecord({ type: 'application', id: application.id })}>
            <td><div className="company-cell"><span className={`company-avatar avatar-${application.companyId.slice(-1)}`}>{getCompanyName(data, application.companyId).slice(0, 1)}</span><span><strong>{getCompanyName(data, application.companyId)}</strong><small>{application.jobTitle}</small></span></div></td>
            <td className="date-cell">{dateLabel(application.appliedAt)}</td>
            <td><span className={`status-badge status-${application.status.toLowerCase()}`}><i />{application.status}</span></td>
            <td className="next-action-cell">{application.nextAction || '—'}</td>
            <td><button className="row-arrow" aria-label={`Edit ${application.jobTitle}`} onClick={(event) => { event.stopPropagation(); setEditRecord({ type: 'application', id: application.id }) }}><ArrowRight size={15} /></button></td>
          </tr>
        ))}</tbody>
      </table></div> : <EmptyState text="Add an application to keep track of your latest opportunities." />}
    </article>
    {editRecord && <RecordDialog type={editRecord.type} recordId={editRecord.id} onClose={() => setEditRecord(null)} />}
  </div>
}

function MetricCard({ label, value, icon, tone }: { label: string; value: number; icon: ReactNode; tone: string }) {
  return <article className="metric-card"><div className="metric-top"><span>{label}</span><i className={`metric-icon ${tone}`}>{icon}</i></div><div className="metric-value">{value}</div></article>
}

function PanelHeading({ title, subtitle, trailing }: { title: string; subtitle: string; trailing?: ReactNode }) {
  return <div className="panel-heading"><div><h2>{title}</h2><p>{subtitle}</p></div>{trailing}</div>
}

function EmptyState({ text }: { text: string }) {
  return <div className="empty-state"><span><Clock3 size={16} /></span><p>{text}</p></div>
}

FILE: src/pages/DirectoryPages.tsx

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { format, isAfter, isBefore, isSameDay, parseISO, startOfDay, startOfToday } from 'date-fns'
import { ArrowDownUp, ArrowRight, CalendarDays, Check, Circle, Download, Pencil, Plus, Search, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { useLocation, useOutletContext, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { getCompanyName } from '../data/demo'
import { useCareerData } from '../context/useCareerData'
import type { AppShellContext, RecordType } from '../types'
import { RecordDialog } from '../components/RecordDialog'
import { MonthlyCalendar } from '../components/MonthlyCalendar'
import { useTheme } from '../context/useTheme'

interface DirectoryRow {
  id: string
  type: RecordType
  name: string
  subtitle: string
  company: string
  status: string
  date: string
  location: string
  tags?: string[]
}

const routeConfig: Record<string, { type: RecordType; title: string; description: string; addLabel: string }> = {
  '/companies': { type: 'company', title: 'Companies', description: 'Keep the organizations on your radar in one place.', addLabel: 'Add company' },
  '/contacts': { type: 'contact', title: 'Contacts', description: 'Build meaningful professional connections over time.', addLabel: 'Add contact' },
  '/applications': { type: 'application', title: 'Applications', description: 'A clear view of every opportunity in your search.', addLabel: 'Add application' },
}

export function DirectoryPage() {
  const { pathname } = useLocation()
  const config = routeConfig[pathname] ?? routeConfig['/applications']
  const { data, deleteRecord } = useCareerData()
  const { openAdd } = useOutletContext<AppShellContext>()
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams()
  const query = searchParams.get('q') ?? ''
  const [sortAscending, setSortAscending] = useState(config.type !== 'application')
  const [sortField, setSortField] = useState(config.type === 'application' ? 'date' : 'name')
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('All')
  const [companyFilter, setCompanyFilter] = useState('All companies')
  const [industryFilter, setIndustryFilter] = useState('All industries')
  const [tagFilter, setTagFilter] = useState('All tags')
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const rows: DirectoryRow[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (config.type === 'company') return data.companies.map((company) => ({
      id: company.id, type: 'company', name: company.name, subtitle: company.industry || 'Industry not set', company: company.size || 'Size not set',
      status: '', date: company.createdAt, location: company.location, tags: company.tags,
    }))
    if (config.type === 'contact') return data.contacts.map((contact) => ({
      id: contact.id, type: 'contact', name: contact.name, subtitle: contact.jobTitle || 'Role not set', company: getCompanyName(data, contact.companyId),
      status: contact.status, date: contact.lastContactedAt, location: '', tags: contact.tags,
    }))
    return data.applications.map((application) => ({
      id: application.id, type: 'application', name: application.jobTitle, subtitle: application.nextAction || 'No next action yet', company: getCompanyName(data, application.companyId),
      status: application.status, date: application.appliedAt, location: application.location,
    }))
  }, [config.type, data])
  const filteredRows = rows.filter((row) => `${row.name} ${row.subtitle} ${row.company} ${row.location} ${(row.tags ?? []).join(' ')}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.trim().toLowerCase()))
    .filter((row) => statusFilter === 'All' || row.status === statusFilter)
    .filter((row) => config.type === 'company' ? industryFilter === 'All industries' || row.subtitle === industryFilter : companyFilter === 'All companies' || row.company === companyFilter)
    .filter((row) => tagFilter === 'All tags' || row.tags?.includes(tagFilter))
    .sort((first, second) => {
      const firstValue = sortField === 'date' ? first.date : first.name
      const secondValue = sortField === 'date' ? second.date : second.name
      return sortAscending ? firstValue.localeCompare(secondValue) : secondValue.localeCompare(firstValue)
    })
  const statusOptions = config.type === 'application' ? ['Saved', 'Applied', 'Assessment', 'Interview', 'Offer', 'Rejected'] : config.type === 'contact' ? ['Connected', 'Requested', 'Not connected'] : []
  const industryOptions = [...new Set(data.companies.map((company) => company.industry).filter(Boolean))]
  const tagOptions = [...new Set(rows.flatMap((row) => row.tags ?? []))]

  const remove = (row: DirectoryRow) => {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(`Delete ${row.name}? This action cannot be undone.`)
    if (confirmed) deleteRecord(row.type, row.id)
  }

  return (
    <section className="directory-page">
      <div className="page-title-row"><div><p className="eyebrow">YOUR CAREER WORKSPACE</p><h1>{config.title}</h1><p className="page-description">{config.description}</p></div><button className="button button-primary" onClick={() => openAdd(config.type)}><Plus size={16} /> {config.addLabel}</button></div>
      <div className="panel directory-panel">
        <div className="directory-toolbar">
          <label className="table-search directory-search"><Search size={15} /><input placeholder={`Search ${config.title.toLowerCase()}...`} aria-label={`Search ${config.title}`} value={query} onChange={(event) => setSearchParams(event.target.value ? { q: event.target.value } : {})} /></label>
          <div className="directory-filters">
            {config.type === 'company' ? <select aria-label="Filter by industry" value={industryFilter} onChange={(event) => setIndustryFilter(event.target.value)}><option>All industries</option>{industryOptions.map((industry) => <option key={industry}>{industry}</option>)}</select> : <select aria-label="Filter by company" value={companyFilter} onChange={(event) => setCompanyFilter(event.target.value)}><option>All companies</option>{data.companies.map((company) => <option key={company.id}>{company.name}</option>)}</select>}
            {(config.type === 'company' || config.type === 'contact') && <select aria-label="Filter by tag" value={tagFilter} onChange={(event) => setTagFilter(event.target.value)}><option>All tags</option>{tagOptions.map((tag) => <option key={tag}>{tag}</option>)}</select>}
            {statusOptions.length > 0 && <select aria-label="Filter by status" value={statusFilter} onChange={(event) => setStatusFilter(event.target.value)}><option value="All">All statuses</option>{statusOptions.map((status) => <option key={status}>{status}</option>)}</select>}
            <select aria-label="Sort records" value={sortField} onChange={(event) => setSortField(event.target.value)}>{config.type === 'company' ? <><option value="name">Name</option><option value="date">Date added</option></> : config.type === 'contact' ? <><option value="name">Name</option><option value="date">Last contacted</option></> : <><option value="date">Application date</option><option value="name">Role</option></>}</select>
            <button className="sort-button" aria-label="Toggle sort direction" onClick={() => setSortAscending((value) => !value)}><ArrowDownUp size={14} /></button>
          </div>
        </div>
        {filteredRows.length ? <div className="directory-table-wrap"><table className="directory-table"><thead><tr><th>{config.type === 'application' ? 'Role' : 'Name'}</th><th>{config.type === 'company' ? 'Industry' : 'Company'}</th><th>{config.type === 'application' ? 'Status' : config.type === 'contact' ? 'Connection' : 'Size'}</th><th>{config.type === 'company' ? 'Location' : config.type === 'contact' ? 'Last contacted' : 'Applied'}</th><th /></tr></thead>
          <tbody>{filteredRows.map((row) => <tr key={row.id} onClick={() => setEditing(row.id)}>
            <td><div className="company-cell"><span className={`company-avatar avatar-${row.id.charCodeAt(row.id.length - 1) % 7}`}>{row.name.slice(0, 1)}</span><span><strong>{row.name}</strong><small>{row.type === 'company' ? row.location : row.subtitle}</small></span></div></td>
            <td>{row.type === 'company' ? row.subtitle : row.company}</td>
            <td>{row.status ? <span className={`status-badge status-${row.status.toLowerCase()}`}><i />{row.status}</span> : row.company}</td>
            <td className="date-cell">{row.type === 'company' ? row.location : row.date ? format(parseISO(row.date), 'MMM d, yyyy') : '—'}</td>
            <td><div className="row-actions"><button className="row-action-button" onClick={(event) => { event.stopPropagation(); setEditing(row.id) }}>Edit</button><button className="row-delete-button" aria-label={`Delete ${row.name}`} onClick={(event) => { event.stopPropagation(); remove(row) }}><Trash2 size={14} /></button></div></td>
          </tr>)}</tbody></table></div> : <div className="directory-empty"><span><Search size={20} /></span><h3>{rows.length ? 'No matching records' : `No ${config.title.toLowerCase()} yet`}</h3><p>{rows.length ? 'Try adjusting your search or filters.' : `Add your first ${config.type} to start organizing your career search.`}</p>{!rows.length && <button className="button button-secondary" onClick={() => openAdd(config.type)}><Plus size={15} /> {config.addLabel}</button>}</div>}
      </div>
      {editing && <RecordDialog type={config.type} recordId={editing} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} />}
    </section>
  )
}

export function TasksPage() {
  const { data, toggleTask, deleteRecord } = useCareerData()
  const { openAdd } = useOutletContext<AppShellContext>()
  const today = startOfToday()
  const open = data.tasks.filter((task) => !task.done).sort((first, second) => first.dueAt.localeCompare(second.dueAt))
  const completed = data.tasks.filter((task) => task.done)
  const [timeFilter, setTimeFilter] = useState('All open')
  const [priorityFilter, setPriorityFilter] = useState('All priorities')
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const visibleOpen = open.filter((task) => {
    const dueDate = parseISO(task.dueAt)
    const matchesTime = timeFilter === 'All open'
      || (timeFilter === 'Overdue' && isBefore(dueDate, today))
      || (timeFilter === 'Today' && isSameDay(dueDate, today))
      || (timeFilter === 'Upcoming' && isAfter(startOfDay(dueDate), today))
    return matchesTime && (priorityFilter === 'All priorities' || task.priority === priorityFilter)
  })
  const handleDelete = (id: string) => {
    if (window.confirm('Delete this task? This action cannot be undone.')) deleteRecord('task', id)
  }
  return <section className="directory-page">
    <div className="page-title-row"><div><p className="eyebrow">STAY ON TRACK</p><h1>Tasks & follow-ups</h1><p className="page-description">A manageable list of thoughtful next steps.</p></div><button className="button button-primary" onClick={() => openAdd('task')}><Plus size={16} /> Add task</button></div>
    <div className="tasks-layout">
      <div className="panel task-board"><PanelTitle title="To do" count={open.length} /><div className="task-board-filters"><select aria-label="Filter tasks by date" value={timeFilter} onChange={(event) => setTimeFilter(event.target.value)}><option>All open</option><option>Today</option><option>Upcoming</option><option>Overdue</option></select><select aria-label="Filter tasks by priority" value={priorityFilter} onChange={(event) => setPriorityFilter(event.target.value)}><option>All priorities</option><option>High</option><option>Medium</option><option>Low</option></select></div><div className="full-task-list">{visibleOpen.map((task) => {
        const overdue = isBefore(parseISO(task.dueAt), today)
        return <div className="full-task-row" key={task.id}><button className="task-check" aria-label="Mark task complete" onClick={() => toggleTask(task.id)}><Check size={12} /></button><div className="full-task-copy"><strong>{task.title}</strong><span>{getCompanyName(data, task.companyId)}</span></div><span className={`priority priority-${task.priority.toLowerCase()}`}>{task.priority}</span><time className={overdue ? 'overdue-date' : ''}>{overdue ? 'Overdue' : format(parseISO(task.dueAt), 'MMM d')}</time><button className="row-delete-button" aria-label={`Edit task ${task.title}`} onClick={() => setEditing(task.id)}><Pencil size={14} /></button><button className="row-delete-button" aria-label={`Delete ${task.title}`} onClick={() => handleDelete(task.id)}><Trash2 size={14} /></button></div>
      })}{!visibleOpen.length && <DirectoryEmpty text={open.length ? 'No tasks match these filters.' : 'Nothing on your list. You’re all caught up.'} />}</div></div>
      <div className="panel task-board"><PanelTitle title="Completed" count={completed.length} /><div className="full-task-list">{completed.map((task) => <div className="full-task-row completed-task" key={task.id}><button className="task-check checked" aria-label="Reopen task" onClick={() => toggleTask(task.id)}><Check size={12} /></button><div className="full-task-copy"><strong>{task.title}</strong><span>{getCompanyName(data, task.companyId)}</span></div><button className="row-delete-button" aria-label={`Edit task ${task.title}`} onClick={() => setEditing(task.id)}><Pencil size={14} /></button><button className="row-delete-button" aria-label={`Delete ${task.title}`} onClick={() => handleDelete(task.id)}><Trash2 size={14} /></button></div>)}{!completed.length && <DirectoryEmpty text="Completed tasks will appear here." />}</div></div>
    </div>
    {editing && <RecordDialog type="task" recordId={editing} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} />}
  </section>
}

export function CalendarPage() {
  const { data, deleteRecord } = useCareerData()
  const { openAdd } = useOutletContext<AppShellContext>()
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<{ type: 'task' | 'event'; id: string } | null>(null)
  const handleDelete = (id: string) => { if (window.confirm('Delete this event? This action cannot be undone.')) deleteRecord('event', id) }
  return <section className="directory-page">
    <div className="page-title-row"><div><p className="eyebrow">PLAN AHEAD</p><h1>Calendar</h1><p className="page-description">Interviews, assessments, tasks and the conversations that move you forward.</p></div><button className="button button-primary" onClick={() => openAdd('event', new Date())}><Plus size={16} /> Add event</button></div>
    <div className="panel calendar-page-panel"><MonthlyCalendar data={data} onAdd={openAdd} onOpen={(type, id) => setEditing({ type, id })} onDeleteEvent={handleDelete} /></div>
    {editing && <RecordDialog type={editing.type} recordId={editing.id} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} />}
  </section>
}

export function SettingsPage() {
  const { data } = useCareerData()
  const { theme, toggleTheme } = useTheme()
  const exportDemoData = () => {
    const file = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' })
    const downloadUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file)
    const link = document.createElement('a')
    link.href = downloadUrl
    link.download = `careeros-demo-${format(new Date(), 'yyyy-MM-dd')}.json`
    link.click()
    URL.revokeObjectURL(downloadUrl)
  }
  return <section className="directory-page"><div className="page-title-row"><div><p className="eyebrow">PREFERENCES</p><h1>Settings</h1><p className="page-description">Manage this local demo workspace.</p></div></div>
    <div className="settings-grid">
      <article className="panel settings-card"><div className="settings-icon"><Circle size={18} /></div><div><h2>Demo workspace</h2><p>Your records are saved only in this browser using local storage. No account or server sync is configured.</p><span className="settings-status"><i /> Local demo mode</span></div></article>
      <article className="panel settings-card"><div className="settings-icon"><CalendarDays size={18} /></div><div><h2>Workspace summary</h2><p>{data.companies.length} companies · {data.contacts.length} contacts · {data.applications.length} applications</p><span className="settings-status">Sample data can be edited or removed at any time.</span></div></article>
      <article className="panel settings-card"><div className="settings-icon"><CalendarDays size={18} /></div><div><h2>Appearance</h2><p>Choose the display theme for this browser.</p><button className="button button-secondary" onClick={toggleTheme}>Switch to {theme === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light'} mode</button></div></article>
      <article className="panel settings-card"><div className="settings-icon"><Download size={18} /></div><div><h2>Export demo data</h2><p>Download a JSON backup of the records saved in this browser. Nothing is uploaded.</p><button className="button button-secondary" onClick={exportDemoData}>Download backup</button></div></article>
      <article className="panel settings-card"><div className="settings-icon"><ArrowRight size={18} /></div><div><h2>Coming next</h2><p>Secure accounts, shared database storage, reminders and calendar integrations are planned for a future milestone.</p><span className="settings-status">No backend authentication or sync is active.</span></div></article>
    </div>
  </section>
}

function PanelTitle({ title, count }: { title: string; count: number }) {
  return <div className="panel-heading"><div><h2>{title}</h2><p>{count} {count === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'}</p></div></div>
}

function DirectoryEmpty({ text }: { text: string }) {
  return <div className="empty-state"><span><Circle size={17} /></span><p>{text}</p></div>
}

export type ApplicationStatus = 'Saved' | 'Applied' | 'Assessment' | 'Interview' | 'Offer' | 'Rejected'
export type TaskPriority = 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High'

export interface Company {
  id: string
  name: string
  industry: string
  location: string
  website: string
  size: string
  tags: string[]
  careersUrl: string
  linkedinUrl: string
  notes: string
  createdAt: string
}

export interface Application {
  id: string
  companyId: string
  jobTitle: string
  status: ApplicationStatus
  appliedAt: string
  nextAction: string
  nextFollowUpAt: string
  location: string
  jobDescription: string
  jobUrl: string
  applicationUrl: string
  jobType: string
  workArrangement: string
  resumeReference: string
  notes: string
}

export interface Contact {
  id: string
  companyId: string
  name: string
  jobTitle: string
  status: string
  lastContactedAt: string
  tags: string[]
  linkedinUrl: string
  email: string
  phone: string
  connectionRequestedAt: string
  connectionAcceptedAt: string
  nextFollowUpAt: string
  notes: string
}

export interface FollowUpTask {
  id: string
  title: string
  companyId: string
  dueAt: string
  priority: TaskPriority
  done: boolean
  description: string
  contactId: string
  applicationId: string
  category: string
}

export interface CareerEvent {
  id: string
  title: string
  eventType: string
  companyId: string
  startsAt: string
  location: string
  endsAt: string
  meetingUrl: string
  notes: string
  contactId: string
  applicationId: string
}

export interface Activity {
  id: string
  description: string
  occurredAt: string
  companyId: string
}

export interface CareerData {
  companies: Company[]
  applications: Application[]
  contacts: Contact[]
  tasks: FollowUpTask[]
  events: CareerEvent[]
  activities: Activity[]
}

export type RecordType = 'company' | 'application' | 'contact' | 'task' | 'event'
export interface AppShellContext {
  openAdd: (type: RecordType, initialDate?: Date) => void
}
